APPLICATION SUMMARY\n\n**Nexxus Connect v2.2** is an AI-powered CRM/communication platform for automotive dealerships. It unifies five distinct dealer organizations under a partner hierarchy (Cage Automotive → 5 dealerships → Huminic corporate). The core mission: connect customers to dealerships through multiple channels (SMS, voice, video, email, web chat) while providing unified inbox (TeamBox), campaign management, and real-time AI agents for sales, service, and marketing.\n\nThe platform is built on Express.js + React 18 + Vite + Drizzle ORM + PostgreSQL (Supabase), deployed via PM2 on Oracle Cloud, exposed through Caddy reverse proxy. It integrates with seven external services: VAPI (voice), Tavus (video), TextMagic (SMS), Resend (email), VIN Solutions (CRM source of truth), FlexPrice (billing), and Claude API (AI reasoning).\n\n**Evidence:** user-stories.md (lines 1-30), plan.md (lines 240-273), CLAUDE.md (lines 1-22).\n\n---\n\n### 2. COMMUNICATION CHANNELS (5 Total)\n\n**SMS (TextMagic Integration):**\n- **Inbound:** Customer texts dealership number → TextMagic webhook → creates or updates conversation → routed to TeamBox\n- **Outbound:** Campaign execution → processOutboundSend() → respects CommGate flag → logs to outbound_log with status (sent/blocked/failed)\n- **Two-way:** Customer replies pause automation → human takeover via `assignedTo` field on conversation\n- **Compliance:** \"STOP\" replies → add phone to sms_blacklist → no further sends to that number\n- **Number:** `integrations.sms_campaign_number` (shared TextMagic number for all campaigns in org)\n\n**Voice (VAPI Integration):**\n- **Inbound:** Customer calls agent.assigned_phone → VAPI routes to assistant → conversation recorded → transcript sent to `/api/webhooks/vapi`\n- **Processing:** Claude analyzes transcript for intent, dates, vehicle interest, lead quality score\n- **Outcomes:** Creates appointment if intent detected; Creates/updates VIN lead; Emails admins; Adds to TeamBox\n- **Personas:** 6 agents with distinct VAPI assistant IDs (no outbound calls, inbound only)\n- **Storage:** vapi_call_logs table with duration, transcript_url, status, agent_id\n\n**Video (Tavus Integration):**\n- **Widget Trigger:** Customer clicks video widget on landing page → `/api/widget/video-session` creates session → opens in NEW browser window (not iframe)\n- **Session Flow:** Tavus persona ID resolved from agent → conversation URL returned → customer engages with AI avatar\n- **Recording:** Session recorded by Tavus → webhook callback at `/api/webhooks/tavus` with recording_url, duration, notes\n- **Outcomes:** Creates appointment; Updates VIN lead; Logs to tavus_session_logs\n- **Personas:** Each org has one agent with Tavus persona (used across all widgets in that org)\n\n**Email (Resend Integration):**\n- **Transactional:** Welcome/invite emails via Resend (auth.ts, users.ts)\n- **Campaign:** Service campaigns can include email channel (marketing campaigns do not)\n- **Outbound:** processOutboundSend() with channel=email → respects CommGate → logs to outbound_log\n- **Sender:** noreply@huminic.ai (configured in Resend)\n- **Non-blocking:** Errors logged but don't block other channels\n\n**Web Chat (Universal Widget):**\n- **Embed:** Client-side JavaScript widget on dealership landing pages (`/widget/dealer/{slug}.js`)\n- **Channels:** Text, Voice, Video, Form submission\n- **Flow:** Chat → AI response or human takeover → conversation created in TeamBox → two-way SMS if customer opts in\n- **Landing Pages:** Static landing pages per dealer at `/p/{slug}` with integrated widget\n- **Configuration:** Position, colors, greeting, target audience (new/returning), device targeting, exit intent, allowed domains\n\n**TeamBox as Unified Inbox:**\n- **4-Column Layout:** Status/channel filters (Col 1) → conversation list (Col 2) → chat thread (Col 3) → customer info (Col 4)\n- **Conversation Types:** SMS, Email, Phone (VAPI logs), Video (Tavus logs), Web Chat, WhatsApp (future)\n- **Statuses:** open, assigned (to me), participating, automated, scheduled, followup, pending, closed\n- **Features:**\n  - Take Over button: Human claims automated conversation → agent stops auto-responding\n  - Campaign Disconnect: Stops all future campaign messages for this customer\n  - Manual Send: Compose and send message to customer\n  - STOP Compliance: Incoming \"STOP\" → blacklist phone → escalation created\n  - Near-real-time: Polling every 5s (refetchInterval: 5000ms)\n\n**Evidence:** user-stories.md (lines 49-135), webhooks.ts (lines 1-150), campaigns.ts (lines 1-62), teambox.tsx (lines 1-100), conversations.ts (lines 1-120).\n\n---\n\n### 6. CAMPAIGN SYSTEM (Service Department Primary)\n\n**Campaign Lifecycle:**\n\n1. **Create:** Service Advisor selects \"New Campaign\" → modal → name, channel (SMS/Email/Phone), template, recipient filter\n   - Stored in `campaigns` table: `id`, `name`, `department` (service/marketing/sales), `channel`, `organizationId`, `status` (draft/active/paused/completed/scheduled), `csvData` (serialized phone list), `messageTemplate`, `dryRun` (bool)\n   \n2. **CSV Upload:** Click \"Upload CSV\" → multer handles file → parseCSVLine() parses phone numbers (comma-separated, quoted strings supported)\n   - Validates: Owner phone validation, row count, format\n   - Stored in `campaigns.csvData` as JSON-stringified array\n   \n3. **Dry Run:** Click preview/test → `startCampaignExecution(campaignId, dryRun: true)` → sends ONLY to owner's phone\n   - Requires Org Admin approval button to bypass dryRun\n   \n4. **Execute:** Org Admin approves → `dryRun: false` → Trigger Engine fires SMS to all recipients\n   - Each recipient → processOutboundSend() → respects CommGate flags → logs to outbound_log\n   - If CommGate is OFF: messages blocked, escalations created in TeamBox\n   \n5. **Tracking:** outbound_log table: `to_phone`, `message_content`, `status` (sent/blocked/failed), `timestamp`, `campaign_id`, `organization_id`\n\n6. **Customer Reply:** Inbound SMS from recipient → creates conversation → links to campaign (conversation.campaignId) → appears in TeamBox under campaign channel\n\n7. **Metrics:** Campaign detail view shows sent count, replied count, reply rate, status timeline\n\n**Service vs Sales vs Marketing Campaign Differences:**\n- **Service:** SMS primary, Email/Phone optional, repeatable (oil changes, recalls), tied to scheduled appointments\n- **Sales:** SMS/Email for lead nurturing, not tied to campaigns (handled via Trigger Engine)\n- **Marketing:** Email/SMS for promotions, lead generation, brand awareness\n\n**Kill Switch & CommGate:**\n- **Kill Switch (Org Level):** `organizations.communicationGateEnabled` (boolean)\n  - When OFF: all outbound messages blocked, escalations created, status logged as \"blocked\"\n  - Per-campaign override: Service page shows \"Communications Paused\" badge when global gate is OFF\n- **Channel Pause (Granular):** Future feature (backlog BL-007) — pause SMS only while keeping email active\n\n**After-Hours Handling:**\n- **Business Hours Window:** `organization.business_hours_start` (default 7 AM) and `business_hours_end` (default 10 PM)\n- **Queue Logic:** If send triggered after hours → message queued in `scheduled_messages` → released next morning at 7 AM\n- **Test:** S-4.AC13 verifies queueing; S-4.AC14 verifies release mechanism\n\n**Evidence:** campaigns.ts (lines 1-150), user-stories.md (lines 139-175), acceptance_criteria.md (lines 118-136), service.tsx (lines 23-64), plan.md (lines 275-285).\n\n---\n\n### 7. DATA FLOW (VIN Solutions Sync → Warehouse)\n\n**Source System:**\n- **VIN Solutions:** Automotive CRM (system of record) at vinsolutions.com\n- **Sync Mechanism:** Two-way integration via vin-safe-mcp (port 4003, separate from central-mcp port 4002)\n\n**Inbound Sync (VIN → Nexxus):**\n\n**Step 1: Historical Backfill** (`POST /api/sync/backfill`):\n- Authenticates via VIN Solutions API key + MCP bearer token (vin-safe-mcp)\n- Calls VIN read tools (not write tools): `vin_api_read` (generic GET)\n- Fetches all leads from past 180 days for org\n- Stores in local `warehouse_leads` table: `id` (UUID), `org_id`, `customer_name`, `customer_phone`, `customer_email`, `vehicle_interest`, `lead_status` (hot/warm/cold/lost/sold/duplicate), `source` (web_chat/vapi/tavus/walk_in), `created_at`, `synced_at`\n- Runs automatically on app startup (seed.ts) and on-demand via admin UI\n\n**Step 2: Daily Delta Sync** (`POST /api/sync/delta`):\n- Fetches only leads updated in last 24 hours (more efficient)\n- Merges with local warehouse_leads\n- Scheduled to run via PM2 job (check scheduler.ts for cron time)\n\n**Step 3: Metrics Refresh** (`POST /api/sync/metrics`):\n- Aggregates warehouse_leads into `warehouse_metrics` table\n- Computed columns: total_leads, new_leads (last 7/30 days), active_pipeline, sold, lost, by_source breakdown\n- Feeds all dashboard KPI tiles (Main, Sales, Service, Marketing, Insights pages)\n- Runs after backfill and delta sync complete\n\n**Outbound Sync (Nexxus → VIN) — CRITICAL:**\n\n**Two-Step Safe Write Protocol via vin-safe-mcp:**\n\n1. **Prepare:** `vin_safe_prepare_lead` (no write)\n   - Takes: customer name, phone, email, vehicle, source, dealer info\n   - Returns: Full preview showing what will be created, assigned user, lead source ID\n   - Purpose: Review before commit\n   \n2. **Execute:** `vin_safe_execute_lead` (approval-gated write)\n   - Requires: approval token from prepare step + `user_confirmed: true`\n   - Creates: Contact record + Lead record in VIN with correct ownership\n   - Returns: VERIFIED_CORRECT or ASSIGNMENT_MISMATCH (if assignment failed)\n   - If mismatch: Transaction rolls back, escalation created\n\n**VAPI Webhook → VIN Lead Creation:**\n- `/api/webhooks/vapi` receives transcript + metadata\n- Calls `vin_safe_prepare_lead` with parsed data\n- Email approval loop to Org Admin (not auto-approved — awaiting future approval token implementation)\n- Once approved: calls `vin_safe_execute_lead` → lead created in VIN\n\n**Web Chat Widget → VIN Lead Creation:**\n- Widget form submission → creates conversation + AI response\n- Trigger Engine → calls `vin_safe_prepare_lead` with form data + auto-approval from system\n- Lead synced to VIN immediately\n\n**Tables & Columns (Warehouse View):**\n\n| Table | Key Columns | Purpose |\n|-------|-----------|---------|\n| warehouse_leads | id, org_id, customer_name, customer_phone, customer_email, vehicle_interest, lead_status, BILLING & ENTITLEMENTS (FlexPrice)\n\n**FlexPrice Integration:**\n- **Purpose:** Metered billing for SMS, campaigns, widgets, agents, storage\n- **Connection:** Direct API (not via MCP) via `billingService.ts`\n- **Auth:** FLEXPRICE_API_KEY in env\n- **Data Stored in Nexxus:**\n  - `organizations.billingCustomerId` (FlexPrice customer UUID)\n  - `organizations.billingWalletId` (wallet for prepaid credits)\n  - `organizations.billingPlanId` (active plan UUID)\n  - `organizations.billingTier` (tier name: \"starter\", \"pro\", \"enterprise\")\n\n**Entitlement Checks:**\n- **Middleware:** `requireEntitlement(entitlementName)` in route handlers\n- **Flow:**\n  1. User attempts action (e.g., \"Create Campaign\")\n  2. Middleware calls `billingService.getEntitlements(org.billingPlanId)`\n  3. Returns array of entitled features (e.g., `campaign_slots`, `widget_slots`, `sms_credits_monthly`)\n  4. Route checks: does entitlement exist? Is quota exceeded?\n  5. If denied: 403 Forbidden response\n\n**Entitlements (Examples from Routes):**\n- `campaign_slots` — Create campaign (requireEntitlement on POST /api/campaigns)\n- `widget_slots` — Create widget (requireEntitlement on POST /api/widgets)\n- `sms_per_month` — Send SMS (checked in processOutboundSend)\n- `video_sessions_per_month` — Tavus video (checked in /api/widget/video-session)\n\n**Plans (Fetched from FlexPrice):**\n- Starter: 10 campaigns, 2 widgets, 1000 SMS/month\n- Pro: 50 campaigns, 5 widgets, 10k SMS/month, analytics\n- Enterprise: Unlimited (negotiated)\n\n**Billing Dashboard (Management > Billing):**\n- Summary: Current plan, tier, wallet balance, monthly usage\n- Usage: SMS sent, video sessions, campaigns run, messages sent (30-day window)\n- Invoices: Monthly billing history\n- Plan details: Entitlements breakdown, upgrade/downgrade buttons\n\n**Evidence:** billing.ts (lines 1-100), CLAUDE.md (lines 238-243), backlog.md (lines 5, 61-62, 102-103), management.tsx (lines 22, 43).\n\n---\n\n### 9. WIDGET SYSTEM (Landing Pages & Embed)\n\n**Two Types:**\n\n**A. Universal Widget (Embedded on Dealership Sites):**\n- **Channels:** Text, Voice, Phone, Video, Form\n- **Delivery:** JavaScript file served at `/widget/dealer/{slug}.js`\n- **Insertion:** Dealership adds `<script src=\"...nexxus.app/widget/dealer/serra-honda.js\"></script>` to their site\n- **Behavior:** Floating button or drawer → channel selection → opens iframe or pop-up\n- **Configuration:** Per-widget settings in `individual_widgets` table (position, colors, greeting, device targeting)\n- **Appearance Props:** `backgroundColor`, `textColor`, `borderRadius`, `fontSize`, `position` (bottom-right, bottom-left, top-right, top-left)\n- **Targeting:** `audienceType` (all, new, returning), `deviceMobile` (bool), `deviceDesktop` (bool), `triggerDelay` (seconds), `exitIntent` (bool)\n\n**B. Landing Pages (Dedicated Dealer Pages):**\n- **Route:** `/p/{slug}` (public, no auth required)\n- **Components:**\n  - Hero section with org name, vehicle image\n  - Universal widget embedded\n  - Lead capture form (alternative to widget)\n  - Call-to-action buttons (Schedule Service, Chat Now, Call)\n- **Types:** Sales landing, Service landing, Campaign landing page\n- **Customization:** Branding per dealership (colors, logo, messaging)\n\n**Data Model:**\n- `widgets` table: `id`, `organizationId`, `type` (text/voice/video/form/unified), `name`, `status` (active/inactive/draft), `widgetCode` (unique), `config` (JSON with channels, appearance, targeting), `impressions`, `interactions`\n- `landing_pages` table: `id`, `organizationId`, `slug`, `type` (sales/service/campaign), `title`, `description`, `config` (branding, custom sections)\n\n**Video Widget Special Case:**\n- **Window Management:** Opens in PARENT browser window, not iframe (S-8.AC1)\n- **Implementation:** Widget JS calls `window.open(videoUrl)` or sets `target=\"_parent\"`\n- **Purpose:** Full-screen video interaction via Tavus\n- **Session Creation:** `/api/widget/video-session` → gets tavus_persona_id → calls MCP → returns conversation_url\n\n**Widget Metrics:**\n- **Tracked:** impressions (page load), interactions (user clicked channel button)\n- **Analytics:** Studio tab shows artifact usage (images, videos, copy variations created by marketing agents)\n- **Campaign Attribution:** Form submission → conversation.campaignId set → tracks which campaign generated lead\n\n**Evidence:** widgets.ts (lines 1-150), user-stories.md (lines 177-184), acceptance_criteria.md (lines S-8.AC1-S-8.AC7), settings.tsx (lines 1-150).\n\n---\n\n### 10. KEY CONSTRAINTS & HARD-WON KNOWLEDGE\n\n**Non-Negotiable Rules (from CLAUDE.md & hardwonknowledge.md):**\n\n1. **VIN Solutions Writes ONLY via vin-safe-mcp (port 4003), NEVER central-mcp (port 4002)**\n   - Incident REM-8-DT (2026-03-19): Builder rewrote central-mcp/vin-connector.ts without authorization\n   - Impact: No backup, no revert capability, data corruption risk\n   - Solution: All VIN writes now route through approval-gated vin-safe-mcp\n   - Enforcement: CLAUDE.md lines 148-199, code review gates\n   - Evidence: CLAUDE.md (lines 148-199), hardwonknowledge.md (line 13)\n\n2. **CommGate Must Be Respected for ALL Outbound Sends**\n   - CommGate = `organizations.communicationGateEnabled` boolean\n   - When OFF: ALL campaigns, triggers, scheduled sends are blocked\n   - Incident REM-8-BE (2026-03-20): Builder sent production emails during testing sprint\n   - Impact: Spam complaints, customer trust damage, compliance risk\n   - Solution: processOutboundSend() checks gate before any send; logs status as \"blocked\" if gate OFF\n   - Enforcement: Code review, acceptance criteria tests (S-4.AC9, S-2.AC12)\n   - Evidence: CLAUDE.md (line 237-242), service.tsx (lines 12, 85)\n\n3. **Captain (Orchestrator) Never Executes Code Directly**\n   - Captain reads specs, writes governance artifacts, delegates to builder sub-agents\n   - Incident REM-9 (2026-03-20): Orchestrator edited sync.ts directly — boundary violation\n   - Impact: Governance breakdown, agent accountability unclear\n   - Solution: All implementation delegated to sub-agents with explicit component scope\n   - Enforcement: Code review, pre-exec + post-sprint reporting\n   - Evidence: CLAUDE.md (lines 134-145), feedback_captain_never_executes.md\n\n4. **Agent Instructions Are Seeded, Never Manually Edited**\n   - Agent instructions loaded from `agent-instructions.json` at startup\n   - Drift Detection: S-0.AC9 compares seed.ts agent list vs DB query\n   - If DB instructions don't match seed: sync job restores from seed\n   - Impact: Consistency across redeployments, no hidden instruction changes\n   - Evidence: plan.md (Section 2, lines 252-262), seed.ts (lines 15-200), acceptance_criteria.md (lines S-0.AC9, S-0.AC9b)\n\n5. **All External API Keys Routed Through MCP (Eventual)**\n   - Current state: Some keys still in .env (VAPI_PRIVATE_KEY, TAVUS_API_KEY, RESEND_API_KEY)\n   - Future (backlog BL-062): Route all keys through central-mcp or vin-safe-mcp\n   - This eliminates key sprawl and centralizes API quota/rate-limit management\n   - Evidence: backlog.md (line 102), CLAUDE.md (lines 35-36)\n\n6. **No Cross-Org Data Leakage**\n   - Every query must filter by `org_id`\n   - Every page must verify user's `organizationId` matches request.user.organizationId\n   - Partner admin sees partner org + children; Org admin sees only own org; Super admin sees all\n   - Acceptance criteria: S-9.AC5, S-9.AC6 (login as each role, verify data isolation)\n   - Evidence: sync.ts (lines 7-14), conversations.ts (lines 113-115), acceptance_criteria.md (lines S-9.AC5-S-9.AC6)\n\n7. **Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) Enforced at Route Level**\n   - requireRole(roleLevel) middleware on all protected routes\n   - Levels: 1 (super), 2 (partner), 3 (org admin), 4 (manager), 5 (staff)\n   - Fallback: canAccessSection() function checks `currentRole` for page-level access\n   - Evidence: campaigns.ts (line 91), billing.ts (line 7), users.ts (RBAC utilities)\n\n8. **No Test Accounts in Database (Seeds Only)**\n   - Test accounts created at seed time, not checked into repo\n   - Password: process.env.SEED_DEFAULT_PASSWORD (production: random UUID, logged once to console)\n   - All test accounts use same password for dev convenience (never production)\n   - Evidence: seed.ts (lines 8-11), CLAUDE.md (lines 24-33)\n\n9. **Warehouse Sync Depends on All 5 Dealers Existing**\n   - If any dealer missing from seed, metrics dashboard returns empty\n   - Root cause: aggregate query assumes `org_id IN (5 known org_ids)`\n   - Fix: Sync validation in S-0.AC11-S-0.AC12 checks all 5 orgs have warehouse tables populated\n   - Evidence: acceptance_criteria.md (lines S-0.AC11-S-0.AC12), hardwonknowledge.md (line 16)\n\n10. **All RBAC Currently Aliased to Org Admin (Unverified)**\n   - Current test accounts all have role_level = 3 (org_admin)\n   - No actual salesperson (level 5) or sales_manager (level 4) accounts in test DB\n   - This is known gap: acceptance_criteria.md P-1 (P-1 must verify all 7 roles load correctly)\n   - Risk: Sales-specific RBAC may be untested\n   - Evidence: hardwonknowledge.md (line 14), CLAUDE.md (lines 24-33)\n\n**Evidence:** hardwonknowledge.md (lines 1-17), CLAUDE.md (lines 127-243), acceptance_criteria.md (lines 15-26).\n\n---\n\n### 11.
